# APROC — joint A process

Verdict: no threshold-qualified finalist. A smaller verified improvement is delivered in best/aproc_best.{qasm,json}.
Best priced admissible circuit: `best/aproc_best.qasm`; L=3.809137946902697e-05, A=0.5067293020027641, J_tau=-3.21032044578e-08.
Required finalist: L < 3.785473322483099e-05, A >= .5002, judge 1e6 PASS.
The strict stop condition is not established: the small improvement is below tau, but misses the finalist margin.

## Method and decisions
Reproduced v36h exactly (L=3.815473322483099e-5, A=.5016415049479505; SHA c8e8a44e104a).
Used the full v36h process, not the old v36c A-only table: 36 physical outputs, coherent records, discarded reset environments, and T-phase labels; boundary.json records the interface.
Actual-state boundary: 212 binary amplitude rows, 75 independent plus-input variables. Equal full column spaces suffice for equality of the complete noiseless instrument, including data and records.
Selected grid CXs jointly across encoder/guards/decoder, retaining the complete exterior and every idle/noise channel in full-circuit prices. Templates explicitly remove guards/checks and pay their cost.
Expanded from 6 to 8 and 9 wires to avoid holding another A interface fixed. Also tested a relaxed record boundary; every emitted relaxed witness passes the exact 15-T ideal accepted-state oracle.
Synthesis: 27 recorded windows/templates; 25 finite-grammar enumerations complete. Diagnostic screens: 24149; all-orders circuits: 1039.
CP-SAT grammar: fixed 47-tick exterior, fixed preparations/readout per template, nearest-neighbour disjoint CXs, baseline CX count +2 (borrowed-wire windows +4). No global optimality claim.
Kept first-order danger/acceptance/idle Pareto data; pair-fault diagnostics only selected full noisy prices. All-orders ranking uses J_tau=A(L-tau).
Reused ACCBUY known-state/pre-role and INTEG2 guard/echo/pair/trade menus on both the best-loss and high-acceptance witnesses; price each conjunction in the greedy pass.
GLOBAL on-grid helpers/late checks were not duplicated; its available results had no better on-grid circuit to combine.

## Measured barriers
Removing q16 MX40 alone: drop278 L=4.4488102373517695e-5 (+6.333369e-6); earlier reset after dropping c55: early16 L=4.82976371773279e-5 (+1.014290e-5). These costs include lost data protection.
Removing q14 echo: drop14pair L=3.827148192391754e-5 (+1.167487e-7), A=.5006456394778994; acceptance falls as well.
Joint q16 witness d278g_0141 replaces CX10->16@39 and MX16/c278@40 by CX22->16@40: L=3.8093541021038275e-5, A=.5030471312053563; gain only 6.119220e-8.
Borrowed-wire, whole-A and decoder family prices are individually listed below; finite search scopes/statuses are in logs/*.synthesis.json. Pair-risk improvements do not imply all-orders gains.
Measured family minima (all orders; admissible prices only): encoder: fulljoint_gain_0000 L=3.80935410211e-05, A=0.503047131, delta=-6.11922e-08; borrowed: borrow17_0000 L=3.81547332247e-05, A=0.501641505, delta=-1.10656e-16; decoder: decoder_0000 L=3.81547332248e-05, A=0.501641505, delta=+0; relaxed: relax14_0016 L=3.81547332248e-05, A=0.501641505, delta=+0
All-A/borrowed/decoder changes did not reach the required margin; the repaired guard gain is only 6.335376e-8, leaving 2.366462e-7 to the finalist threshold. Bounded searches found no larger gain; this is not a proof against other windows or grammars.

## Verified versus inferred
Verified: baseline exact reproduction; saved-circuit all-orders values and SHA/evaluator hashes; coherent-boundary equality for every strong-model witness; exact ideal-state checks for relaxed witnesses; d278g_0141 judge 1e6 PASS (MC L=4.3708725056225084e-5, sem=7.95e-6, A=.503332, 6.5 s).
Not proved: global process optimum, completeness beyond recorded finite grammars, or absence of a threshold-qualified circuit. Low-order screening/Pareto pruning is diagnostic, not an all-orders exclusion proof.
Final combined circuit: judge 1e6 PASS (MC L=3.652019764336129e-5, sem=7.32e-6, A=.506569), 353 CX, 7.8 s; all 1,039 price/hash/log checks pass (audit.json). No APROC jobs remain.
No submission, git mutation, GPU use, other-lane writes, or sub-agents. Host use restricted to zebra and tom after 16:40 KST; only our own job PIDs were interrupted to reorder/reroute work.
